  261:     "free_text",
  262:     "free_text_manual",
  263:     "app_action",
  264:     "debug",
  265:     "debug_free_text",
  266:   ]);
  267: 
  268:   return (
  269:     directlyAllowedSources.has(normalizedSource) ||
  270:     normalizedSource.includes("manual") ||
  271:     normalizedSource.includes("chat") ||
  272:     normalizedSource.includes("free_text") ||
  273:     normalizedSource.includes("debug")
  274:   );
  275: }
  276: 
  277: function getCategoryDerivationPolicyForCompleteRoute(params: {
  278:   event: ActivityEventRow;
  279:   completedStatus: string;
  280: }): CategoryDerivationCompleteRoutePolicy {
  281:   const { event, completedStatus } = params;
  282: 
  283:   if (event.status !== completedStatus) {
  284:     return {
  285:       enabled: false,
  286:       reason: "not_completed_status",
  287:       inputText: null,
  288:       actorId: null,
  289:     };
  290:   }
  291: 
  292:   if (!isCategoryDerivationSourceAllowedForCompleteRoute(event.source)) {
  293:     return {
  294:       enabled: false,
  295:       reason: "source_not_allowed",
  296:       inputText: null,
  297:       actorId: null,
  298:     };
  299:   }
  300: 
  301:   const inputText = getCategoryDerivationInputTextForCompleteRoute(event);
  302: 
  303:   if (inputText === null) {
  304:     return {
  305:       enabled: false,
  306:       reason: "missing_textual_input",
  307:       inputText: null,
  308:       actorId: null,
  309:     };
  310:   }
  311: 
  312:   const actorId =
  313:     event.performed_by_actor_id ??
  314:     event.acting_as_actor_id ??
  315:     event.acting_for_actor_id;
  316: 
  317:   if (!isUuidLikeForCategoryDerivation(actorId)) {
  318:     return {
  319:       enabled: false,
  320:       reason: "missing_actor_id",
  321:       inputText,
  322:       actorId: null,
  323:     };
  324:   }
  325: 
  326:   return {
  327:     enabled: true,
  328:     reason: null,
  329:     inputText,
  330:     actorId,
  331:   };
  332: }
  333: 
  334: function buildAdditionalCategoryLinksForCompleteRoute(params: {
  335:   enabled: boolean;
  336:   activityEventId: string;
  337:   derivationRunId: string | null;
  338:   resolutionResult: unknown;
  339: }): AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] {
  340:   const { enabled, activityEventId, derivationRunId, resolutionResult } = params;
  341: 
  342:   if (!enabled) {
  343:     return [];
  344:   }
  345: 
  346:   const resolutionRecord = asRecordForCategoryDerivation(resolutionResult);
  347:   const possibleCandidates = resolutionRecord.candidates;
  348:   const resolvedCandidates = Array.isArray(possibleCandidates)
  349:     ? possibleCandidates
  350:     : [];
  351: 
  352:   const allowedResolutionStatuses = new Set([
  353:     "resolved_existing",
  354:     "created_suggested",
  355:     "created_active",
  356:   ]);
  357: 
  358:   const links: AdditionalValueObjectCategoryLink[] = [];
  359: 
  360:   for (const candidate of resolvedCandidates) {
  361:     const categoryId = readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  362:       "categoryId",
  363:       "category_id",
  364:       "resolvedCategoryId",
  365:       "resolved_category_id",
  366:     ]);
  367: 
  368:     if (!isUuidLikeForCategoryDerivation(categoryId)) {
  369:       continue;
  370:     }
  371: 
  372:     const resolutionStatus =
  373:       readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  374:         "resolutionStatus",
  375:         "resolution_status",
  376:       ]) ?? "resolved_existing";
  377: 
  378:     if (!allowedResolutionStatuses.has(resolutionStatus)) {
  379:       continue;
  380:     }
  381: 
  382:     const candidateSlug =
  383:       readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  384:         "candidateSlug",
  385:         "candidate_slug",
  386:         "categorySlug",
  387:         "category_slug",
  388:         "slug",
  389:       ]) ?? categoryId;
  390: 
  391:     links.push({
  392:       categoryId,
  393:       categoryTable: "contextual_categories",
  394:       categoryRole: "semantic_component",
  395:       source: "rule",
  396:       confidence: readNumberFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  397:         "confidence",
  398:         "score",
  399:       ]),
  400:       derivationRunId,
  401:       activityCategoryDerivationId: null,
  402:       activityEventId,
  403:       candidateSlug,
  404:       candidateTitle: readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  405:         "candidateTitle",
  406:         "candidate_title",
  407:         "title",
  408:         "label",
  409:         "name",
  410:       ]),
  411:       semanticLayer: readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(candidate, [
  412:         "semanticLayer",
  413:         "semantic_layer",
  414:       ]),
  415:     });
  416:   }
  417: 
  418:   return links;
  419: }

  421: async function runCategoryDerivationForCompleteRoute(params: {

  474:         defaultCategoryType: "derived",
  475:       }
  476:     );
  477: 
  478:     const persistenceResult = await persistCategoryDerivations(
  479:       supabase as unknown as CategoryDerivationPersistenceSupabaseClient,
  480:       {
  481:         activityEventId: event.id,
  482:         input: derivationInput,
  483:         derivationResult: extractionResult,
  484:         resolvedCandidates: resolutionResult.candidates,
  485:         actorId: policy.actorId,
  486:         organizationId: null,
  487:         modelName: null,
  488:         promptVersion: null,
  489:         needsUserConfirmation:
  490:           resolutionResult.unresolvedCount > 0 ||
  491:           extractionResult.candidates.some((candidate) =>
  492:             Boolean((candidate as { needsUserReview?: unknown }).needsUserReview)
  493:           ),
  494:       }
  495:     );
  496:

  421: async function runCategoryDerivationForCompleteRoute(params: {
  422:   event: ActivityEventRow;
  423:   completedStatus: string;
  424: }): Promise<CategoryDerivationCompleteRouteResult> {
  425:   const policy = getCategoryDerivationPolicyForCompleteRoute(params);
  426: 
  427:   if (!policy.enabled || policy.inputText === null || policy.actorId === null) {
  428:     return {
  429:       enabled: false,
  430:       ok: null,
  431:       skipped: true,
  432:       reason: policy.reason,
  433:       error: null,
  434:       derivationRunId: null,
  435:       extractionCandidateCount: 0,
  436:       resolutionCandidateCount: 0,
  437:       resolutionUnresolvedCount: 0,
  438:       persistenceDerivationRowsCreated: 0,
  439:       additionalCategoryLinks: [],
  440:       warnings: [],
  441:       errors: [],
  442:       policy,
  443:     };
  444:   }
  445: 
  446:   try {
  447:     const { event } = params;
  448: 
  449:     const derivationInput: CategoryDerivationInput = {
  450:       activityEventId: event.id,
  451:       inputText: policy.inputText,
  452:       title: event.title,
  453:       description: event.description,
  454:       durationMinutes: event.duration_minutes ?? 0,
  455:       inputLanguage: null,
  456:       actorId: policy.actorId,
  457:       organizationId: null,
  458:       metadata: {
  459:         endpoint: "/api/activity/complete",
  460:         p4Step: "P4.10.0-C8-P3-B7-C1-B",
  461:         featureFlag: "categoryDerivation",
  462:       },
  463:     };
  464: 
  465:     const extractionResult = deriveCategoryCandidates(derivationInput);
  466: 
  467:     const resolutionResult = await resolveCategoryCandidates(
  468:       supabase as unknown as CategoryResolverSupabaseClient,
  469:       extractionResult.candidates,
  470:       {
  471:         createPolicy: CATEGORY_DERIVATION_COMPLETE_ROUTE_CREATE_POLICY,
  472:         dryRun: false,
  473:         sourceType: "rule",
  474:         defaultCategoryType: "derived",
  475:       }
  476:     );
  477: 
  478:     const persistenceResult = await persistCategoryDerivations(
  479:       supabase as unknown as CategoryDerivationPersistenceSupabaseClient,
  480:       {
  481:         activityEventId: event.id,
  482:         input: derivationInput,
  483:         derivationResult: extractionResult,
  484:         resolvedCandidates: resolutionResult.candidates,
  485:         actorId: policy.actorId,
  486:         organizationId: null,
  487:         modelName: null,
  488:         promptVersion: null,
  489:         needsUserConfirmation:
  490:           resolutionResult.unresolvedCount > 0 ||
  491:           extractionResult.candidates.some((candidate) =>
  492:             Boolean((candidate as { needsUserReview?: unknown }).needsUserReview)
  493:           ),
  494:       }
  495:     );
  496: 
  497:     const persistenceOk =
  498:       readBooleanFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, ["ok"]) ?? false;
  499:     const derivationRunId = readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(
  500:       persistenceResult,
  501:       ["derivationRunId", "derivation_run_id", "runId", "run_id"]
  502:     );
  503: 
  504:     const persistenceDerivationRowsCreated =
  505:       readNumberFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, [
  506:         "derivationRowsCreated",
  507:         "derivation_rows_created",
  508:       ]) ?? 0;
  509: 
  510:     const additionalCategoryLinks = buildAdditionalCategoryLinksForCompleteRoute({
  511:       enabled: true,
  512:       activityEventId: event.id,
  513:       derivationRunId,
  514:       resolutionResult,
  515:     });
  516: 
  517:     const warnings = [
  518:       ...extractionResult.warnings,
  519:       ...resolutionResult.warnings,
  520:       ...readStringArrayFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, ["warnings"]),
  521:     ];
  522: 
  523:     const errors = [
  524:       ...extractionResult.errors,
  525:       ...resolutionResult.errors,
  526:       ...readStringArrayFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, ["errors"]),
  527:     ];
  528: 
  529:     const ok = extractionResult.ok && resolutionResult.ok && persistenceOk;
  530: 
  531:     return {
  532:       enabled: true,
  533:       ok,
  534:       skipped: false,
  535:       reason: null,
  536:       error: null,
  537:       derivationRunId,
  538:       extractionCandidateCount: extractionResult.candidates.length,
  539:       resolutionCandidateCount: resolutionResult.candidates.length,
  540:       resolutionUnresolvedCount: resolutionResult.unresolvedCount,
  541:       persistenceDerivationRowsCreated,
  542:       additionalCategoryLinks,
  543:       warnings,
  544:       errors,
  545:       policy,
  546:     };
  547:   } catch (error) {
  548:     const message =
  549:       error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown Category Derivation error";
  550: 
  551:     return {
  552:       enabled: true,
  553:       ok: false,
  554:       skipped: false,
  555:       reason: "exception",
  556:       error: message,
  557:       derivationRunId: null,
  558:       extractionCandidateCount: 0,
  559:       resolutionCandidateCount: 0,
  560:       resolutionUnresolvedCount: 0,
  561:       persistenceDerivationRowsCreated: 0,
  562:       additionalCategoryLinks: [],
  563:       warnings: [],
  564:       errors: [message],
  565:       policy,
  566:     };
  567:   }
  568: }
  569: 
  570: function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  571:   if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)) {
  572:     return value as Record<string, unknown>;
  573:   }
  574: 
  575:   return {};
  576: }
  577: 
  578: function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
  579:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  580:     return null;
  581:   }
  582: 
  583:   const trimmed = value.trim();
  584: 
  585:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
  586: }
  587: 
  588: function asNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
  589:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  590:     return value;
  591:   }
  592: 
  593:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  594:     const normalized = value.trim().replace(",", ".");
  595:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  596: 
  597:     if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  598:       return parsed;
  599:     }
  600:   }
  601: 
  602:   return null;
  603: }
  604: 
  605: function resolveCompletionTiming(params: {
  606:   event: ActivityEventRow;
  607:   body: ActivityCompleteBody;
  608: }): CompletionTiming {
  609:   const { event, body } = params;
  610: 
  611:   if (!event.started_at) {
  612:     return {
  613:       ok: false,
  614:       error: "Cannot complete activity event without started_at.",
  615:     };
  616:   }
  617: 
  618:   const startedDate = new Date(event.started_at);
  619: 
  620:   if (Number.isNaN(startedDate.getTime())) {
  621:     return {
  622:       ok: false,
  623:       error: "Stored started_at is invalid.",
  624:     };
  625:   }
  626: 
  627:   const rawEndedAt = asString(body.endedAt) ?? asString(body.endTime);
  628:   const explicitDurationMinutes = asNumber(body.durationMinutes);
  629: 
  630:   if (explicitDurationMinutes !== null && explicitDurationMinutes < 0) {
  631:     return {
  632:       ok: false,
  633:       error: "durationMinutes must be greater than or equal to 0.",
  634:     };
  635:   }
  636: 
  637:   if (rawEndedAt) {
  638:     const endedDate = new Date(rawEndedAt);
  639: 
  640:     if (Number.isNaN(endedDate.getTime())) {
  641:       return {
  642:         ok: false,
  643:         error: "Invalid endedAt or endTime.",
  644:       };
  645:     }

  578: function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
  579:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  580:     return null;
  581:   }
  582: 
  583:   const trimmed = value.trim();
  584: 
  585:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;

  588: function asNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
  589:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  590:     return value;
  591:   }
  592: 
  593:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  594:     const normalized = value.trim().replace(",", ".");
  595:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  596: 
  597:     if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  598:       return parsed;
  599:     }
  600:   }
  601: 
  602:   return null;
  603: }

  627:   const rawEndedAt = asString(body.endedAt) ?? asString(body.endTime);

  629: 
  630:   if (explicitDurationMinutes !== null && explicitDurationMinutes < 0) {
  631:     return {
  632:       ok: false,
  633:       error: "durationMinutes must be greater than or equal to 0.",
  634:     };
  635:   }

  640:     if (Number.isNaN(endedDate.getTime())) {
  641:       return {
  642:         ok: false,

  116: function asRecordForCategoryDerivation(
  117:   value: unknown
  118: ): Record<string, unknown> {
  119:   if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)) {
  120:     return value as Record<string, unknown>;
  121:   }
  122: 
  123:   return {};

  126: function asStringForCategoryDerivation(value: unknown): string | null {
  127:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  128:     return null;
  129:   }
  130: 
  131:   const trimmed = value.trim();
  132:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
  133: }
  134: 
  135: function asNumberForCategoryDerivation(value: unknown): number | null {
  136:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  137:     return value;
  138:   }
  139: 
  140:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  141:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(value.trim().replace(",", "."));
  142:     return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
  143:   }
  144: 
  145:   return null;
  146: }
  147: 
  148: function asBooleanForCategoryDerivation(value: unknown): boolean | null {
  149:   if (typeof value === "boolean") {
  150:     return value;
  151:   }
  152: 
  153:   return null;
  154: }
  155: 
  156: function readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(
  157:   value: unknown,
  158:   fieldNames: string[]
  159: ): string | null {
  160:   const record = asRecordForCategoryDerivation(value);
  161: 
  162:   for (const fieldName of fieldNames) {
  163:     const parsed = asStringForCategoryDerivation(record[fieldName]);
  164: 
  165:     if (parsed !== null) {
  166:       return parsed;
  167:     }
  168:   }
  169: 
  170:   return null;
  171: }

  183:       return parsed;

  190: function readBooleanFieldForCategoryDerivation(

  207: function readStringArrayFieldForCategoryDerivation(
  208:   value: unknown,
  209:   fieldNames: string[]
  210: ): string[] {
  211:   const record = asRecordForCategoryDerivation(value);
  212: 
  213:   for (const fieldName of fieldNames) {
  214:     const possibleArray = record[fieldName];
  215: 
  216:     if (Array.isArray(possibleArray)) {
  217:       return possibleArray
  218:         .filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
  219:         .map((item) => item.trim())
  220:         .filter((item) => item.length > 0);
  221:     }
  222:   }
  223: 
  224:   return [];
  225: }
  226: 
  227: function isUuidLikeForCategoryDerivation(value: unknown): value is string {
  228:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  229:     return false;
  230:   }
  231: 
  232:   return /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(
  233:     value.trim()
  234:   );
  235: }

  421: async function runCategoryDerivationForCompleteRoute(params: {
  422:   event: ActivityEventRow;
  423:   completedStatus: string;
  424: }): Promise<CategoryDerivationCompleteRouteResult> {
  425:   const policy = getCategoryDerivationPolicyForCompleteRoute(params);
  426: 
  427:   if (!policy.enabled || policy.inputText === null || policy.actorId === null) {
  428:     return {
  429:       enabled: false,
  430:       ok: null,

  440:       warnings: [],
  441:       errors: [],

  443:     };
  444:   }
  445:

  448:

  451:       inputText: policy.inputText,
  452:       title: event.title,
  453:       description: event.description,
  454:       durationMinutes: event.duration_minutes ?? 0,
  455:       inputLanguage: null,
  456:       actorId: policy.actorId,
  457:       organizationId: null,
  458:       metadata: {
  459:         endpoint: "/api/activity/complete",
  460:         p4Step: "P4.10.0-C8-P3-B7-C1-B",
  461:         featureFlag: "categoryDerivation",
  462:       },
  463:     };
  464: 
  465:     const extractionResult = deriveCategoryCandidates(derivationInput);
  466: 
  467:     const resolutionResult = await resolveCategoryCandidates(
  468:       supabase as unknown as CategoryResolverSupabaseClient,
  469:       extractionResult.candidates,
  470:       {
  471:         createPolicy: CATEGORY_DERIVATION_COMPLETE_ROUTE_CREATE_POLICY,
  472:         dryRun: false,
  473:         sourceType: "rule",
  474:         defaultCategoryType: "derived",
  475:       }
  476:     );
  477: 
  478:     const persistenceResult = await persistCategoryDerivations(
  479:       supabase as unknown as CategoryDerivationPersistenceSupabaseClient,
  480:       {
  481:         activityEventId: event.id,
  482:         input: derivationInput,
  483:         derivationResult: extractionResult,
  484:         resolvedCandidates: resolutionResult.candidates,
  485:         actorId: policy.actorId,
  486:         organizationId: null,
  487:         modelName: null,
  488:         promptVersion: null,
  489:         needsUserConfirmation:
  490:           resolutionResult.unresolvedCount > 0 ||
  491:           extractionResult.candidates.some((candidate) =>
  492:             Boolean((candidate as { needsUserReview?: unknown }).needsUserReview)
  493:           ),
  494:       }
  495:     );
  496: 
  497:     const persistenceOk =
  498:       readBooleanFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, ["ok"]) ?? false;
  499:     const derivationRunId = readStringFieldForCategoryDerivation(
  500:       persistenceResult,
  501:       ["derivationRunId", "derivation_run_id", "runId", "run_id"]
  502:     );
  503: 
  504:     const persistenceDerivationRowsCreated =
  505:       readNumberFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, [
  506:         "derivationRowsCreated",
  507:         "derivation_rows_created",
  508:       ]) ?? 0;
  509: 
  510:     const additionalCategoryLinks = buildAdditionalCategoryLinksForCompleteRoute({
  511:       enabled: true,
  512:       activityEventId: event.id,
  513:       derivationRunId,
  514:       resolutionResult,
  515:     });
  516: 
  517:     const warnings = [
  518:       ...extractionResult.warnings,
  519:       ...resolutionResult.warnings,
  520:       ...readStringArrayFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, ["warnings"]),
  521:     ];
  522: 
  523:     const errors = [
  524:       ...extractionResult.errors,
  525:       ...resolutionResult.errors,
  526:       ...readStringArrayFieldForCategoryDerivation(persistenceResult, ["errors"]),
  527:     ];
  528:

  530:

  546:     };
  547:   } catch (error) {

  550:

  578: function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
  579:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  580:     return null;
  581:   }
  582:

  584:

  588: function asNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
  589:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  590:     return value;
  591:   }
  592: 
  593:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  594:     const normalized = value.trim().replace(",", ".");
  595:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  596: 
  597:     if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  598:       return parsed;
  599:     }
  600:   }
  601: 
  602:   return null;
  603: }

  605: function resolveCompletionTiming(params: {
  606:   event: ActivityEventRow;
  607:   body: ActivityCompleteBody;
  608: }): CompletionTiming {
  609:   const { event, body } = params;
  610: 
  611:   if (!event.started_at) {
  612:     return {
  613:       ok: false,
  614:       error: "Cannot complete activity event without started_at.",
  615:     };
  616:   }
  617: 
  618:   const startedDate = new Date(event.started_at);
  619: 
  620:   if (Number.isNaN(startedDate.getTime())) {
  621:     return {
  622:       ok: false,
  623:       error: "Stored started_at is invalid.",
  624:     };
  625:   }
  626: 
  627:   const rawEndedAt = asString(body.endedAt) ?? asString(body.endTime);
  628:   const explicitDurationMinutes = asNumber(body.durationMinutes);
  629: 
  630:   if (explicitDurationMinutes !== null && explicitDurationMinutes < 0) {
  631:     return {
  632:       ok: false,
  633:       error: "durationMinutes must be greater than or equal to 0.",
  634:     };
  635:   }
  636: 
  637:   if (rawEndedAt) {
  638:     const endedDate = new Date(rawEndedAt);
  639: 
  640:     if (Number.isNaN(endedDate.getTime())) {
  641:       return {
  642:         ok: false,
  643:         error: "Invalid endedAt or endTime.",
  644:       };

  588: function asNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
  589:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
  590:     return value;
  591:   }
  592: 
  593:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  594:     const normalized = value.trim().replace(",", ".");
  595:     const parsed = Number.parseFloat(normalized);
  596: 
  597:     if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
  598:       return parsed;
  599:     }
  600:   }
  601: 
  602:   return null;
  603: }

  605: function resolveCompletionTiming(params: {
  606:   event: ActivityEventRow;
  607:   body: ActivityCompleteBody;
  608: }): CompletionTiming {
  609:   const { event, body } = params;
  610: 
  611:   if (!event.started_at) {
  612:     return {
  613:       ok: false,
  614:       error: "Cannot complete activity event without started_at.",
  615:     };
  616:   }
  617: 
  618:   const startedDate = new Date(event.started_at);
  619: 
  620:   if (Number.isNaN(startedDate.getTime())) {
  621:     return {
  622:       ok: false,
  623:       error: "Stored started_at is invalid.",
  624:     };
  625:   }
  626: 
  627:   const rawEndedAt = asString(body.endedAt) ?? asString(body.endTime);
  628:   const explicitDurationMinutes = asNumber(body.durationMinutes);
  629: 
  630:   if (explicitDurationMinutes !== null && explicitDurationMinutes < 0) {
  631:     return {
  632:       ok: false,
  633:       error: "durationMinutes must be greater than or equal to 0.",
  634:     };
  635:   }
  636: 
  637:   if (rawEndedAt) {
  638:     const endedDate = new Date(rawEndedAt);
  639: 
  640:     if (Number.isNaN(endedDate.getTime())) {
  641:       return {
  642:         ok: false,
  643:         error: "Invalid endedAt or endTime.",
  644:       };
  645:     }
  646: 
  647:     if (endedDate.getTime() < startedDate.getTime()) {
  648:       return {
  649:         ok: false,
  650:         error: "endedAt must be greater than or equal to startedAt.",
  651:       };
  652:     }
  653: 
  654:     return {
  655:       ok: true,
  656:       startedAt: startedDate.toISOString(),
  657:       endedAt: endedDate.toISOString(),
  658:       durationMinutes: Math.round(
  659:         (endedDate.getTime() - startedDate.getTime()) / 60000
  660:       ),
  661:     };
  662:   }
  663: 
  664:   if (explicitDurationMinutes !== null) {
  665:     const endedDate = new Date(
  666:       startedDate.getTime() + explicitDurationMinutes * 60000
  667:     );
  668: 
  669:     return {
  670:       ok: true,
  671:       startedAt: startedDate.toISOString(),
  672:       endedAt: endedDate.toISOString(),
  673:       durationMinutes: explicitDurationMinutes,
  674:     };
  675:   }
  676: 
  677:   const endedDate = new Date();
  678: 
  679:   if (endedDate.getTime() < startedDate.getTime()) {
  680:     return {
  681:       ok: false,

  696: async function getExistingImpactEventsCount(eventId: string) {

  696: async function getExistingImpactEventsCount(eventId: string) {
  697:   const { count, error } = await supabase

  720:       ? "Complete a previously started activity event and process rule-based impacts."
  721:       : ACTIVITY_RECORDING_DISABLED_MESSAGE,
  722:     example: {
  723:       eventId: "activity-event-uuid",

  726:     deterministicTestExample: {

  734: export async function POST(request: Request) {
  735:   if (!ACTIVITY_RECORDING_ENABLED) {
  736:     return NextResponse.json(
  737:       {
  738:         ok: false,
  739:         error: ACTIVITY_RECORDING_DISABLED_MESSAGE,
  740:       },
  741:       { status: 503 }
  742:     );
  743:   }
  744: 
  745:   const { appUser, errorResponse } = await getActivityUserContext();
  746: 
  747:   if (errorResponse) {
  748:     return errorResponse;
  749:   }
  750: 
  751:   if (!appUser) {
  752:     return NextResponse.json(
  753:       {
  754:         ok: false,
  755:         error: "User context not found",
  756:       },
  757:       { status: 500 }
  758:     );
  759:   }
  760: 
  761:   let body: ActivityCompleteBody;
  762: 
  763:   try {
  764:     body = (await request.json()) as ActivityCompleteBody;
  765:   } catch {
  766:     return NextResponse.json(
  767:       {
  768:         ok: false,
  769:         error: "Invalid JSON body",
  770:       },
  771:       { status: 400 }
  772:     );
  773:   }
  774: 
  775:   const eventId = asString(body.eventId);
  776: 
  777:   if (!eventId) {
  778:     return NextResponse.json(
  779:       {
  780:         ok: false,
  781:         error: "eventId is required.",
  782:       },
  783:       { status: 400 }
  784:     );
  785:   }
  786: 
  787:   const { data: eventData, error: eventError } = await supabase
  788:     .from("activity_events")
  789:     .select("*")
  790:     .eq("id", eventId)
  791:     .eq("user_id", appUser.id)
  792:     .maybeSingle();
  793: 
  794:   if (eventError) {
  795:     return NextResponse.json(
  796:       {
  797:         ok: false,
  798:         error: eventError.message,
  799:       },
  800:       { status: 500 }
  801:     );
  802:   }
  803: 
  804:   if (!eventData) {
  805:     return NextResponse.json(
  806:       {
  807:         ok: false,
  808:         error: "Activity event not found or access denied.",
  809:       },
  810:       { status: 404 }
  811:     );
  812:   }
  813: 
  814:   const event = eventData as ActivityEventRow;
  815:   const completedStatus = ACTIVITY_STATUS_COMPLETED;
  816: 
  817:   if (event.status === completedStatus) {
  818:     try {
  819:       const existingImpactEventsCount = await getExistingImpactEventsCount(
  820:         event.id
  821:       );
  822: 
  823:       const impactResult = await processActivityImpacts({
  824:         eventId: event.id,
  825:         userId: appUser.id,
  826:         activityTemplateId: event.activity_template_id,
  827:         activityTypeId: event.activity_type_id,
  828:         durationMinutes: event.duration_minutes,
  829:         startedAt: event.started_at,
  830:       });
  831: 
  832:       return NextResponse.json({
  833:         ok: true,
  834:         status: "already_completed",
  835:         event,
  836:         impactEvents: impactResult.impactEvents,
  837:         dailyAggregates: impactResult.dailyAggregates,
  838:         currentSnapshots: impactResult.currentSnapshots,
  839:         impactProcessor: {
  840:           ok: impactResult.ok,
  841:           skipped: impactResult.skipped,
  842:           reason: impactResult.reason,
  843:           counts: impactResult.counts,
  844:           existingImpactEventsCount,
  845:         },
  846:         lifecycle: {
  847:           alreadyCompleted: true,
  848:           note:
  849:             "Activity event was already completed. Duplicate impact processing was skipped if impacts already existed.",
  850:         },
  851:       });
  852:     } catch (error) {
  853:       return NextResponse.json(
  854:         {
  855:           ok: false,
  856:           error:
  857:             error instanceof Error
  858:               ? error.message
  859:               : "Failed to inspect completed activity event.",
  860:         },

  867:     return NextResponse.json(

  870:         error: `Activity event status '${event.status}' cannot be completed by this endpoint.`,
  871:         allowedStatuses: Array.from(ACTIVITY_COMPLETABLE_STATUSES),
  872:       },
  873:       { status: 409 }
  874:     );
  875:   }

  696: async function getExistingImpactEventsCount(eventId: string) {
  697:   const { count, error } = await supabase
  698:     .from("impact_events")
  699:     .select("id", {
  700:       count: "exact",
  701:       head: true,
  702:     })
  703:     .eq("event_id", eventId);
  704: 
  705:   if (error) {
  706:     throw new Error(error.message);
  707:   }
  708: 
  709:   return count ?? 0;
  710: }

  731:   });

  734: export async function POST(request: Request) {
  735:   if (!ACTIVITY_RECORDING_ENABLED) {
  736:     return NextResponse.json(
  737:       {
  738:         ok: false,
  739:         error: ACTIVITY_RECORDING_DISABLED_MESSAGE,
  740:       },
  741:       { status: 503 }
  742:     );
  743:   }
  744: 
  745:   const { appUser, errorResponse } = await getActivityUserContext();
  746: 
  747:   if (errorResponse) {
  748:     return errorResponse;
  749:   }
  750: 
  751:   if (!appUser) {
  752:     return NextResponse.json(
  753:       {
  754:         ok: false,
  755:         error: "User context not found",
  756:       },
  757:       { status: 500 }
  758:     );
  759:   }
  760: 
  761:   let body: ActivityCompleteBody;
  762: 
  763:   try {
  764:     body = (await request.json()) as ActivityCompleteBody;
  765:   } catch {
  766:     return NextResponse.json(
  767:       {
  768:         ok: false,
  769:         error: "Invalid JSON body",
  770:       },
  771:       { status: 400 }
  772:     );
  773:   }
  774: 
  775:   const eventId = asString(body.eventId);
  776: 
  777:   if (!eventId) {
  778:     return NextResponse.json(
  779:       {
  780:         ok: false,
  781:         error: "eventId is required.",
  782:       },
  783:       { status: 400 }
  784:     );
  785:   }
  786: 
  787:   const { data: eventData, error: eventError } = await supabase
  788:     .from("activity_events")
  789:     .select("*")
  790:     .eq("id", eventId)
  791:     .eq("user_id", appUser.id)
  792:     .maybeSingle();
  793: 
  794:   if (eventError) {
  795:     return NextResponse.json(
  796:       {
  797:         ok: false,
  798:         error: eventError.message,
  799:       },
  800:       { status: 500 }
  801:     );
  802:   }
  803: 
  804:   if (!eventData) {
  805:     return NextResponse.json(
  806:       {
  807:         ok: false,
  808:         error: "Activity event not found or access denied.",
  809:       },
  810:       { status: 404 }
  811:     );
  812:   }
  813: 
  814:   const event = eventData as ActivityEventRow;
  815:   const completedStatus = ACTIVITY_STATUS_COMPLETED;
  816: 
  817:   if (event.status === completedStatus) {
  818:     try {
  819:       const existingImpactEventsCount = await getExistingImpactEventsCount(
  820:         event.id
  821:       );
  822: 
  823:       const impactResult = await processActivityImpacts({
  824:         eventId: event.id,
  825:         userId: appUser.id,
  826:         activityTemplateId: event.activity_template_id,
  827:         activityTypeId: event.activity_type_id,
  828:         durationMinutes: event.duration_minutes,
  829:         startedAt: event.started_at,
  830:       });
  831: 
  832:       return NextResponse.json({
  833:         ok: true,
  834:         status: "already_completed",
  835:         event,
  836:         impactEvents: impactResult.impactEvents,
  837:         dailyAggregates: impactResult.dailyAggregates,
  838:         currentSnapshots: impactResult.currentSnapshots,
  839:         impactProcessor: {
  840:           ok: impactResult.ok,
  841:           skipped: impactResult.skipped,
  842:           reason: impactResult.reason,
  843:           counts: impactResult.counts,
  844:           existingImpactEventsCount,
  845:         },
  846:         lifecycle: {
  847:           alreadyCompleted: true,
  848:           note:
  849:             "Activity event was already completed. Duplicate impact processing was skipped if impacts already existed.",
  850:         },
  851:       });
  852:     } catch (error) {
  853:       return NextResponse.json(
  854:         {
  855:           ok: false,
  856:           error:
  857:             error instanceof Error
  858:               ? error.message
  859:               : "Failed to inspect completed activity event.",
  860:         },
  861:         { status: 500 }
  862:       );
  863:     }
  864:   }
  865: 
  866:   if (!isCompletableActivityStatus(event.status)) {
  867:     return NextResponse.json(
  868:       {
  869:         ok: false,
  870:         error: `Activity event status '${event.status}' cannot be completed by this endpoint.`,
  871:         allowedStatuses: Array.from(ACTIVITY_COMPLETABLE_STATUSES),
  872:       },
  873:       { status: 409 }
  874:     );
  875:   }
  876: 
  877:   const timing = resolveCompletionTiming({
  878:     event,
  879:     body,
  880:   });
  881:

  883:     return NextResponse.json(

  885:         ok: false,
  886:         error: timing.error,
  887:       },
  888:       { status: 400 }
  889:     );
  890:   }

  897:   const processingStartedAt = new Date();

  904:     rawPayload: {
  905:       endpoint: "/api/activity/complete",

  910:     },

   44: function normalizeEnvText(value: string | undefined): string | null {
   45:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
   46:     return null;
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   const trimmed = value.trim();
   50: 
   51:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function isCategoryDerivationRouteRunnerMode(
   55:   value: string,
   56: ): value is CategoryDerivationRouteRunnerMode {
   57:   return CATEGORY_DERIVATION_ROUTE_RUNNER_MODES.includes(
   58:     value as CategoryDerivationRouteRunnerMode,
   59:   );
   60: }
   61: 
   62: function readBooleanEnv(
   63:   value: string | undefined,
   64:   defaultValue: boolean,
   65:   envName: string,
   66:   warnings: string[],
   67: ): boolean {
   68:   const normalized = normalizeEnvText(value)?.toLowerCase() ?? null;
   69: 
   70:   if (normalized === null) {
   71:     return defaultValue;
   72:   }
   73: 
   74:   if (["true", "1", "yes", "y", "on"].includes(normalized)) {
   75:     return true;
   76:   }
   77: 
   78:   if (["false", "0", "no", "n", "off"].includes(normalized)) {
   79:     return false;
   80:   }
   81: 
   82:   warnings.push(
   83:     `${envName} has unsupported boolean value "${value}". Falling back to ${String(
   84:       defaultValue,
   85:     )}.`,
   86:   );
   87: 
   88:   return defaultValue;
   89: }
   90: 
   91: function readModeEnv(
   92:   value: string | undefined,
   93:   warnings: string[],
   94: ): CategoryDerivationRouteRunnerMode {
   95:   const normalized = normalizeEnvText(value)?.toLowerCase() ?? null;
   96: 
   97:   if (normalized === null) {
   98:     return "disabled";
   99:   }
  100: 
  101:   if (isCategoryDerivationRouteRunnerMode(normalized)) {
  102:     return normalized;
  103:   }
  104: 
  105:   warnings.push(
  106:     `${CATEGORY_DERIVATION_ROUTE_RUNNER_MODE_ENV} has unsupported value "${value}". Falling back to disabled.`,
  107:   );
  108: 
  109:   return "disabled";
  110: }
  111: 
  112: export function getCategoryDerivationRouteRunnerConfig(
  113:   env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env,
  114: ): CategoryDerivationRouteRunnerConfig {
  115:   const warnings: string[] = [];
  116: 
  117:   const raw: CategoryDerivationRouteRunnerRawConfig = {
  118:     mode: env[CATEGORY_DERIVATION_ROUTE_RUNNER_MODE_ENV],
  119:     includeResponseDebug:
  120:       env[CATEGORY_DERIVATION_ROUTE_RUNNER_INCLUDE_RESPONSE_DEBUG_ENV],
  121:     failActivityComplete:
  122:       env[CATEGORY_DERIVATION_ROUTE_RUNNER_FAILS_ACTIVITY_COMPLETE_ENV],
  123:   };
  124: 
  125:   const mode = readModeEnv(raw.mode, warnings);
  126: 
  127:   const includeResponseDebug = readBooleanEnv(
  128:     raw.includeResponseDebug,
  129:     false,
  130:     CATEGORY_DERIVATION_ROUTE_RUNNER_INCLUDE_RESPONSE_DEBUG_ENV,

   87: export interface FindExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunResult {
   88:   ok: boolean;
   89:   found: boolean;

   93:   query: FindExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunQuery | null;
   94:   errors: string[];
   95:   warnings: string[];
   96: }
   97: 
   98: function errorMessage(error: SupabaseErrorLike | string | Error | null): string {
   99:   if (!error) {
  100:     return "Unknown error";
  101:   }
  102: 
  103:   if (typeof error === "string") {
  104:     return error;
  105:   }
  106: 
  107:   if (error instanceof Error) {
  108:     return error.message;
  109:   }
  110: 
  111:   return error.message ?? JSON.stringify(error);
  112: }
  113: 
  114: function normalizeRequiredText(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  115:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  116:     return null;
  117:   }
  118: 
  119:   const trimmed = value.trim();
  120: 
  121:   return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
  122: }
  123: 
  124: function normalizeStatuses(statuses: string[] | undefined): string[] {
  125:   if (!statuses || statuses.length === 0) {
  126:     return [...DEFAULT_COMPLETED_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_RUN_STATUSES];
  127:   }
  128: 
  129:   const normalized = statuses
  130:     .map((status) => normalizeRequiredText(status))
  131:     .filter((status): status is string => status !== null);
  132: 
  133:   if (normalized.length === 0) {
  134:     return [...DEFAULT_COMPLETED_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_RUN_STATUSES];
  135:   }
  136: 
  137:   return Array.from(new Set(normalized));
  138: }
  139: 
  140: export function buildCompletedCategoryDerivationRunLookupQuery(
  141:   input: FindExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunInput,
  142: ): FindExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunQuery | null {
  143:   const activityEventId = normalizeRequiredText(input.activityEventId);
  144: 
  145:   if (!activityEventId) {
  146:     return null;
  147:   }
  148: 
  149:   return {
  150:     activityEventId,
  151:     processorVersion:
  152:       normalizeRequiredText(input.processorVersion) ??
  153:       DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_PROCESSOR_VERSION,
  154:     ruleVersion:
  155:       normalizeRequiredText(input.ruleVersion) ??
  156:       DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_RULE_VERSION,
  157:     schemaVersion:
  158:       normalizeRequiredText(input.schemaVersion) ??
  159:       DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_COMPLETE_ROUTE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
  160:     policyVersion:
  161:       normalizeRequiredText(input.policyVersion) ??
  162:       DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_COMPLETE_ROUTE_POLICY_VERSION,
  163:     statuses: normalizeStatuses(input.statuses),
  164:   };
  165: }
  166: 
  167: export async function findExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunForActivityEvent(
  168:   supabase: CategoryDerivationRunLookupClient,
  169:   input: FindExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunInput,
  170: ): Promise<FindExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunResult> {
  171:   const query = buildCompletedCategoryDerivationRunLookupQuery(input);
  172: 
  173:   if (!query) {
  174:     return {
  175:       ok: false,
  176:       found: false,
  177:       reason: "invalid_lookup_input",
  178:       derivationRunId: null,
  179:       row: null,
  180:       query: null,
  181:       errors: ["activityEventId is required for Category Derivation run lookup."],
  182:       warnings: [],
  183:     };
  184:   }
  185: 
  186:   const { data, error } = await supabase
  187:     .from<CategoryDerivationRunRow>("category_derivation_runs")
  188:     .select("*")
  189:     .eq("activity_event_id", query.activityEventId)
  190:     .eq("processor_version", query.processorVersion)
  191:     .eq("rule_version", query.ruleVersion)
  192:     .eq("schema_version", query.schemaVersion)
  193:     .eq("policy_version", query.policyVersion)
  194:     .in("status", query.statuses)
  195:     .order("finished_at", { ascending: false, nullsFirst: false })
  196:     .order("created_at", { ascending: false, nullsFirst: false })
  197:     .limit(1)
  198:     .maybeSingle();
  199: 
  200:   if (error) {
  201:     return {
  202:       ok: false,
  203:       found: false,
  204:       reason: "lookup_error",
  205:       derivationRunId: null,
  206:       row: null,
  207:       query,
  208:       errors: [errorMessage(error)],
  209:       warnings: [],
  210:     };
  211:   }
  212: 
  213:   if (!data) {
  214:     return {
  215:       ok: true,
  216:       found: false,
  217:       reason: "no_existing_completed_run",
  218:       derivationRunId: null,
  219:       row: null,
  220:       query,
  221:       errors: [],
  222:       warnings: [],
  223:     };
  224:   }
  225: 
  226:   return {
  227:     ok: true,
  228:     found: true,
  229:     reason: "existing_completed_run_found",
  230:     derivationRunId: data.id,
  231:     row: data,
  232:     query,
  233:     errors: [],
  234:     warnings: [],
  235:   };
  236: }
  237: 
  238: export function summarizeExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunLookup(
  239:   result: FindExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunResult,
  240: ) {
  241:   return {
  242:     ok: result.ok,
  243:     found: result.found,
  244:     reason: result.reason,
  245:     derivationRunId: result.derivationRunId,
  246:     query: result.query,
  247:     errors: result.errors,
  248:     warnings: result.warnings,
  249:   };
  250: }

  222: export async function runCategoryDerivationForCompleteRoute(
  223:   params: RunCategoryDerivationForCompleteRouteInput,
  224: ): Promise<CategoryDerivationCompleteRouteIntegrationResult> {
  225:   const config = params.config ?? getCategoryDerivationRouteRunnerConfig();
  226: 
  227:   if (config.isDisabled) {
  228:     return baseResult({
  229:       config,
  230:       ok: true,
  231:       skipped: true,
  232:       reason: "disabled",
  233:     });
  234:   }
  235: 
  236:   if (config.isDebugOnly) {
  237:     return baseResult({
  238:       config,
  239:       ok: true,
  240:       skipped: true,
  241:       reason: "debug_only",
  242:     });
  243:   }
  244: 
  245:   if (config.usesLegacyExistingCompleteRouteFlow) {
  246:     return baseResult({
  247:       config,
  248:       ok: true,
  249:       skipped: true,
  250:       reason: "legacy_existing_mode_selected",
  251:     });
  252:   }
  253: 
  254:   if (!config.usesRouteRunnerInCompleteRoute) {
  255:     return baseResult({
  256:       config,
  257:       ok: true,
  258:       skipped: true,
  259:       reason: "not_route_runner_mode",
  260:     });
  261:   }
  262: 
  263:   const activityEventId = normalizeText(params.activityEvent.id);
  264: 
  265:   if (!activityEventId) {
  266:     return baseResult({
  267:       config,
  268:       ok: !config.failActivityComplete,
  269:       skipped: true,
  270:       reason: "missing_activity_event_id",
  271:       errors: ["activityEvent.id is required for Category Derivation."],
  272:     });
  273:   }
  274: 
  275:   const inputText = getActivityEventInputText(params.activityEvent);
  276: 
  277:   if (!inputText) {
  278:     return baseResult({
  279:       config,
  280:       ok: true,
  281:       skipped: true,
  282:       reason: "missing_input_text",
  283:       derivationRunId: null,
  284:     });
  285:   }
  286: 
  287:   const actorId = normalizeText(params.actorId);
  288: 
  289:   if (!actorId) {
  290:     return baseResult({
  291:       config,
  292:       ok: true,
  293:       skipped: true,
  294:       reason: "missing_actor_id",
  295:       derivationRunId: null,
  296:     });
  297:   }
  298: 
  299:   const schemaVersion =
  300:     normalizeText(params.schemaVersion) ??
  301:     DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_COMPLETE_ROUTE_SCHEMA_VERSION;
  302: 
  303:   const policyVersion =
  304:     normalizeText(params.policyVersion) ??
  305:     DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_COMPLETE_ROUTE_POLICY_VERSION;
  306: 
  307:   const processorVersion =
  308:     normalizeText(params.processorVersion) ??
  309:     DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_PROCESSOR_VERSION;
  310: 
  311:   const ruleVersion =
  312:     normalizeText(params.ruleVersion) ??
  313:     DEFAULT_CATEGORY_DERIVATION_RULE_VERSION;
  314: 
  315:   let idempotency: FindExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunResult | null =
  316:     null;
  317: 
  318:   if (config.shouldPersist) {
  319:     idempotency =
  320:       await findExistingCompletedCategoryDerivationRunForActivityEvent(
  321:         params.supabase,
  322:         {
  323:           activityEventId,
  324:           processorVersion,
  325:           ruleVersion,
  326:           schemaVersion,
  327:           policyVersion,
  328:         },
  329:       );
  330: 
  331:     if (!idempotency.ok) {
  332:       const result = baseResult({
  333:         config,
  334:         ok: !config.failActivityComplete,
  335:         skipped: false,
  336:         reason: "lookup_error",
  337:         warnings: idempotency.warnings,
  338:         errors: idempotency.errors,
  339:         idempotency,
  340:         debug: {
  341:           idempotency,
  342:         },
  343:       });
  344: 
  345:       if (config.failActivityComplete) {
  346:         return result;
  347:       }
  348:     }
  349: 
  350:     if (idempotency.found) {
  351:       return baseResult({
  352:         config,
  353:         ok: true,
  354:         skipped: true,
  355:         reason: "existing_completed_run_found",
  356:         derivationRunId: idempotency.derivationRunId,
  357:         warnings: idempotency.warnings,
  358:         errors: idempotency.errors,
  359:         idempotency,
  360:         debug: {
  361:           idempotency,
  362:         },
  363:       });
  364:     }
  365:   }
  366: 
  367:   const routeRunnerResult = await runCategoryDerivationRoute({
  368:     supabase: params.supabase,
  369:     activityEventId,
  370:     input: {
  371:       activityEventId,
  372:       inputText,
  373:       title: normalizeText(params.activityEvent.title),
  374:       description: normalizeText(params.activityEvent.description),
  375:       durationMinutes: normalizeNumber(params.activityEvent.duration_minutes),
  376:       inputLanguage: normalizeText(params.inputLanguage),
  377:       actorId,
  378:       organizationId: normalizeText(params.organizationId),
  379:       metadata: {

  381:         activityEventSource: normalizeText(params.activityEvent.source),
  382:         activityEventMetadata: toJsonValue(params.activityEvent.metadata_json),
  383:         adapterMetadata: asJsonRecord(params.metadata),
  384:       },
  385:     },
  386:     resolverOptions: {
  387:       createPolicy: "suggested_only",
  388:       defaultStatus: "suggested",
  389:       sourceType: "category_derivation_complete_route",
  390:     },

  392:     modelAlias: null,
  393:     promptVersion: null,
  394:     schemaVersion,
  395:     policyVersion,
  396:     persist: config.shouldPersist,
  397:     resolve: config.shouldResolve,
  398:     metadata: {
  399:       source: "complete_route_integration_adapter",
  400:       schemaVersion,
  401:       policyVersion,
  402:       processorVersion,
  403:       ruleVersion,
  404:       config: getCategoryDerivationRouteRunnerConfigSummary(config),
  405:       adapterMetadata: asJsonRecord(params.metadata),
  406:     },
  407:   });
  408: 
  409:   return mapRouteRunnerResult({
  410:     config,
  411:     routeRunnerResult,
  412:     idempotency,
  413:   });
  414: }

PRE-PATCH DESIGN DECISION

F5 should not immediately remove the legacy complete-route Category Derivation code.
The route already contains old logic using deriveCategoryCandidates, resolveCategoryCandidates, and persistCategoryDerivations.
The first patch should introduce the new support layer in the safest possible way:

1. Add imports only.
2. Add a new adapter result variable in disabled/default-safe mode.
3. Keep legacy logic available under legacy_existing transition mode.
4. Default config remains disabled, so routeRunner does not execute unless env is explicitly changed.
5. Do not enable persistent mode by default.
6. Do not call OpenAI.
7. Do not alter Value Object Bridge, impact events, daily aggregates, or current snapshots.

RECOMMENDED F5-B PATCH SHAPE

A minimal first patch should:
- import getCategoryDerivationRouteRunnerConfig from config.ts;
- import runCategoryDerivationForCompleteRoute from completeRouteIntegration.ts;
- keep existing legacy functions and old categoryDerivationResult behavior;
- add a guarded adapter call only when mode is NOT disabled/debug_only/legacy_existing;
- or add adapter metadata in disabled mode only, without changing existing result shape.

Important:
The exact patch must be generated only after reviewing this inventory output.

POSSIBLE PATCH STRATEGY OPTIONS

Option 1 — safest documentation-only next step:
Create a detailed patch plan document before touching route.ts.

Option 2 — minimal disabled-mode code patch:
Add imports and config summary only, but do not call routeRunner.
This proves config wiring without DB writes.

Option 3 — adapter call behind disabled-by-default config:
Call runCategoryDerivationForCompleteRoute only after legacy result is prepared, but because default mode is disabled it returns skipped/disabled without writes.
This is more useful but must be carefully placed to avoid duplicate old/new result confusion.

Recommended next step:
P4.10.0-C8-E-F5-B — create exact patch plan document for /api/activity/complete.
